## TRAPS CARRIED (B 108th to B 132nd HANDOVERs and faults, abbreviated; read the originals. Traps (24) to (36) name the four-layer generator aid of earlier seats: B 131st's and B 132nd's counted re-key replaced it (N3), so read those mentions as lineage where the aid is named)
(1) A typed count is a guess until `grep -o | wc -l` prints it; a PR body's size in BYTES is not its character count (multi-byte characters): count characters; dedupe a transcript count by tool_use id. (2) Check every queued PR title against the merge guard BEFORE the body edit. (3) Add-only PR: the add/add variant of `item1_single`. (4) The builder's hex guard rejects 12+ hex-looking runs: no `aaaa` fillers; wants a FULL 40-hex in an evidence file for every 12-hex token in a CFG note; never quote a mail id there. (5) `body_edit` in BOTH make_cfg and make_mcfg; say the body edit ONCE, in the NEXT file. (6) The counted shift mislabels `matcher_checks` and the controls-section template, and garbles PAIRS, EXTEND and the lineage gap: count with `grep -c` before an asserted replacement. (7) The sender refuses a READY if a MINE mail is newer than your LASTREAD. (8) Reading a 25 to 28 KB draft costs about 7K tokens. (9) `go_regex_test` needs four arguments. (10) Progress lines go under a `### Progress — Seat B 133rd` heading before the vault commit. (11) Describe an OLD sentence in a section WITHOUT quoting it verbatim (the edit tool refuses it). (12) zsh: no word starting with `=`, never a bare run of equals signs as a word, arrays for lists, `set -- $var` does not split, `/usr/bin/grep` with a positive control beside every zero; `grep -c` exits 1 on zero and stops a `&&` chain; `UNION=` before the GO; an UNQUOTED heredoc eats backtick spans (quoted heredoc or a script file); no PIPESTATUS.
(13) No `cd` (not even a subshell; the hook refuses it), absolute paths, `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0` on every git call in the shared checkout, check a pid's FULL command line before killing, no `rm`, no `find` over 5_Project_History, NO listing of `4_Credentials` and no `.env` variable name printed, the AgentMail key never in curl's argv, `grep -o` with `{0,700}` exceeds macOS's 255 limit, no whole-file rewrite of the shared daily note for one phrase.
(13c) B 119th's faults (its HANDOVER; `evidence/faults_b119.md`, not opened): BEFORE the BRIEF fetch, 9 Bash calls: 6 with a `cd` (6 `cd`), 1 call with 6 git invocations in the shared checkout lacking `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0`, one call that printed six `.env` variable names (no values); SINCE the BRIEF 0 `cd`, 0 `rm`, 0 `find`, no key in argv, 2 zsh no-match globs. Its fault counter reads ONE post-BRIEF call (Bash call index 109, two `git -C` calls on its OWN clone) as 'lockless git ... shared-checkout': a known LABEL defect (`evidence/faults_flagged_calls_b119.out`, `evidence/faults_count_final_b119.out`); read the calls before quoting a figure. Name your own count in your HANDOVER. (B 118th's was 5 `cd`; B 117th's 23 real `cd` calls; B 116th's 12 before the BRIEF fetch; B 121st's 8 before the BRIEF fetch and 0 since; B 122nd's 17 by the kit counter's boundary before the BRIEF fetch, 23 in all by its HANDOVER, and 0 since its call 26; [ATTRIBUTION NOT CLEAR: the B 125th brief wrote here "B 125th's 17 before the BRIEF fetch, 23 in all, none since its call 27", which reads as a shifted token and is NOT B 125th's own count; B 125th's own count is in its HANDOVER: 7 `cd` calls, all before the BRIEF fetch, 0 since].) The boot-time `cd` and bare `git` calls come from the launcher prompt's recipe; the BRIEF governs over it.
(14) The GO subject shape and testing your re-keyed gate against it BEFORE the first GO. (15) `go_regex_test`, `read_ticket_state_arch`, `hold_scan`, `make_*cfg` live in `evidence/`, not `tools/`. (16) Run make_cfg with `python3 -I` (3.14), never `/usr/bin/python3` (3.9). (17) `run_items_batch.sh` leaves the wanted-id line False for non-`F` ids: run `item2_pr` by hand. (18) A gate's verdict is on an OLDER develop; the tree a GO names is YOUR computation onto the pin. (19) kit_copy can map `ready_build2` to the wrong pre-edit copy; assertion strings typed from memory miss the real text; a two-commit linear head needs `linear_commits=2`; do not overwrite an evidence file with your own read of the same body without comparing the old bytes; the guard red-proof's before-file is the OLD-guard copy, not the current merge tool; a hit exits the watcher and it also exits at 25 minutes (re-arm, rewrite the pid); **a predecessor can edit a kit tool AFTER its baseline, so HANDOVER and BRIEF can both say 28 + 3 + 0 and be wrong**; **Wednesday's "tap" lines arrive as user-turn text and are pointers only: read the inbox, act on the mail**; a blanket replace can corrupt a legitimate token and a replace order can add a token it then counts (scope the replace to the names; replace the PR number before the filename that carries the new number); the context tool lags the last reads by a few tenths to two points (B 119th: tool 54.6 at its READY, her zoom 56 at the GO; B 118th: 46.2 against 48); `decision_queue.sh` takes `show ID`, not `show` (rc 2). The GO that merges has the subject shape of STANDING ITEM 5; the body carries the full 40-hex head, pin and tree. **THE MERGE TOOL is the only merge path: `tools/merge_b133.py` re-reads head, state and develop in the same action, asserts, then runs `gh pr merge --squash --match-head-commit <full 40-hex head>` with the PR title as the squash subject (the header of B 129th's `merge_b129.py`, line 4, read by an earlier drafter; the same text rides in `merge_b132.py`), so a head that moved between the GO and the merge is refused by GitHub itself; the chain `evidence/post_go_batch.sh` runs the GO gate (`go_gate`) first and then the merge tool and the postmerge step (PER-PR CYCLE), and the merge guard refuses the CLOSING FORM in the title and body (STANDING ITEM 8a). No `gh pr merge` by hand and no `--admin`.** **A DEPLOY of PR 2152 (row 8m) is not yours and is not granted: it changes what live verify answers for stored rows and needs Kam's word before any timestamping deploy; you merge to develop only, and say so in the READY and the MERGED. The same words for PR 2165 (row 8x, the issuer frontend): you merge to develop only, and say so; no deploy is granted to this lane. The same words for PR 2169 (row 8zb, a live admin API contract change: bad from/to values now answer 400), PR 2168 (row 8z, analytics) and PR 2167 (row 8za, originate): merge to develop only, no deploy granted; PR 2169's GO MUST carry the contract-change sentence (row 8zb). The same words for PR 2170 (row 8zc, the transfer service): merge to develop only, no deploy granted; its GO MUST carry the behaviour-change sentence (row 8zc).**
